### Page 13

Global Market Outlook 13

Expansion at a solid pace  
With economic activity expanding at a solid pace , we expect 
earnings growth to fundamentally drive global equities higher. 
Heading into 2027, rising AI adoption continues to drive 
strong growth  in the technology sector. Recent debates 
about regulating the pace of model developments could 
influence the mix of AI spending, with differing impacts on 
various parts of the AI value chain. However, rising adoption 
and growth in AI use cases should support massive growth in 
capex, benefitting the semiconductor industry, cloud service 
providers and internet platforms. The capex benefits also 
spillover to other industries providing the power infrastructure, 
materials and financing of the investmen ts. The risks are a 
slowdown in AI capex or net job losses from AI disruption, but 
we do not see these having a significant impact yet. 
Rate hikes  from central banks represent a headwind for 
equity valuations, although this has been largely priced in by 
bond markets, given the rise in yields we have seen this year. 
Further rate hikes are being priced by the market. However, 
we expect these headwinds to be offset by solid earnings 
growth in global equities: at 34% in 2026 and 16% in 2027.  
Fig. 17 Global equities continue to be supported by 
strong earnings growth, led by the technology sector  
Earnings growth by sectors for MSCI AC World index

Source: Company reports, Standard Chartered  
US equities – Overweight  
US equity valuations have softened this year  as strong 
earnings growth have outpaced the rise in the market. The 
12m forward P/E of around 20x remains at a premium to other 
markets but justified, in our view, by the high returns on equity, 
differences in sector composition and superior growth outlook. 
In the run-up to US mid-term elections in November, we would 
not be surprised to see higher volatility, though we would use 
any undue corrections as opportunities to add exposure. 
AxJ equities – Overweight 
AxJ enjoys the strongest earnings growth among major 
markets, as its technology sector remains a key 
beneficiary of the AI capex cycle. AxJ also has the lowest 
P/E valuation, given the earnings surge ha s not yet been  
reflected in the market. Within AxJ, we are Overweight 
Taiwan, which benefits from clear visibility on AI -driven 
growth and dominance in manufacturing advanced AI chips. 
We lower China to a Core holding as the valuation re -
rating potential now appears delayed. Share placements for 
AI spending will likely continue, diluting earnings. Regulatory 
concerns still linger from US restrictions on AI chips and 
distillation accusations, even as the Trump-Xi summit signals 
a mutual desire for steady ties. Looming offshore trust taxes 
may lead to further sell down pressures , while domestic 
demand remains weak. However, valuation s remain 
inexpensive with rapid tech innovation and AI -localisation 
investments. Policy stimulus could provide upside. 
South Korea equities remain a Core holding. We are 
positive on the long-term agreements giving greater earnings 
visibility on memory chip s. Positioning is also cleaner, with 
reduced foreign investor exposure and declin ing leveraged 
ETFs. India remains a Core holding amid positive earnings 
revision and strong domestic demand , alongside  macro 
headwinds from RBI rate hikes. We remain Underweight on 
ASEAN, as oil shocks and a supported USD could weigh on 
the region, although Singapore offers defensive growth. 
Fig. 18 Global equity valuations have declined this year, 
with market gains led by earnings growth 
Composition of returns YTD for regional MSCI equity indices

Source: FactSet, Standard Chartered. Min-Max over last 10 years 
Japan equities – Core allocation 
Rising nominal economic growth is supporting solid 
broad-based earnings revisions , with rising rates 
supportive of bank earnings. The fiscal support for strategic 
industries is also pro-growth, offsetting energy shocks.  
Europe ex-UK equities – Core allocation  
Europe ex-UK is seeing an earnings inflection  after three 
stagnant years, with rising rates supportive of banks’ 
earnings. Fiscal expansion is benefitting domestic demand 
and the industrial sector, although earnings growth in the 
region still lags that of the US and AxJ.  
UK equities – Underweight  
The UK lacks exposure to growth sectors, with relatively 
muted earnings growth. We expect this to weigh on the UK , 
making it lag the performance of other markets.

Add Global Semiconductors, remove HSTECH 
We initiate d an Opportunistic Idea on Global 
Semiconductors on 9 Sep t 2026. We expect sustained AI 
investments and rising AI adoption to support earnings 
growth, with demand widening beyond processors to memory, 
networking, and advanced manufacturing equipment . 
Valuations have also eased since the middle of the year. 
We remove Hang Seng Technology (31 Oct 2024 to 24 Sep 
2026: -1.2%) amid mixed earnings trends, while AI 
monetisation has yet to keep pace with rising investments. 
Widening US-China tech restrictions adds to the uncertainty, 
and we see better risk-adjusted opportunities elsewhere.

Ongoing Opportunistic Ideas 
Euro area banks: A higher-for-longer rates backdrop should 
extend the earnings upcycle, with further ECB tightening  
expected. Corporate loan demand is showing recovery, while 
non-interest income  is growing . Strong capital generation 
supports higher dividends and continued share buybacks. 
Global Power & Electrification: Rising AI and data centre 
power demand should drive a multi -year grid and 
electrification investment cycle. Power availability and grid 
capacity are emerging bottlenecks to the AI infra buildout. 
Addressing these constraints requires capex and time, which 
supports strong earnings visibility for grid equipment makers. 
Japan Banks : BoJ policy normali sation drives net interest 
margin upside, while Japan’s reflation cycle supports nominal 
loan demand, corporate capex and wealth activity. 
Governance reform is a re-rating driver  independent of the 
rate cycle. This includes recycling legacy holdings towards 
growth, buybacks and dividends.  
MSCI Taiwan: Taiwan’s foundry leadership makes it a critical 
supplier to global tech firms, while its broadening AI hardware 
supply chain is driving strong revenue growth.  
Global High Dividend  payers: Provides a higher -income 
equity allocation with historically lower volatility than the MSCI 
ACWI and modest downside cushioning on balance.  
US Communication Services: We remain constructive and 
expect further upward earnings revisions, driven by improving 
digital ad-spending and AI monetisation. 
Sector views: Continue to broaden & diversify 
We upgrade US Materials to Overweight as robust capex 
drives demand for industrial metals and construction 
materials, while we remain bullish on gold . US Technology 
and Communication Services remain the primary growth 
engines, supported by structural AI tailwinds. We remain 
Overweight in Financials across the US, Europe ex -UK, and 
Japan, underpinned by earnings stability  and elevated rates 
supporting interest income. This is further supported by a US 
capital markets recovery  and European balance -sheet 
strength. We remain Overweight in Europe ex-UK Industrials, 
reflecting EU fiscal stimulus and Germany's infrastructure 
acceleration legislation. 
In China, we broaden our Overweight exposure by upgrading 
three sectors: Materials (benefiting from structurally elevated 
commodity prices), Healthcare (biotech innovation and a robust 
pipeline sustaining growth), and Financials (easing margin 
pressure, fee recovery, and capital market reform). Our existing 
Overweight positio ns in Technology and Communication 
Services maintain exposure to China's AI build-out, with fiscal 
deployment into high-tech infrastructure serving as a near-term 
catalyst. On the other hand, we downgrade Utilities to 
Underweight on capped margins and retain our Underweight 
views on Real Estate and Consumer Staples.

Our view

• We maintain our Overweight on gold, and our 3 - and 12-month price targets at USD 4,750/oz and 5,000/oz, respectively.

Gold outlook:  Gold has remained resilient despite a more 
hawkish Fed and higher real yields, suggesting much of the 
tightening is already reflected in prices. With markets 
discounting an aggressive Fed path, we see an increasingly 
asymmetric risk -reward: limited downsid e risk from further 
rate repricing, but meaningful upside should Fed rate 
expectations shift towards being less restrictive. Official -
sector demand has slowed from recent years but has become 
broader based, while investor positioning remains well below 
levels seen at the start of the 2022 hiking cycle. 
Oil outlook: Oil prices have risen sharply as recent attacks 
cast greater uncertainty over Saudi export s, even as Gulf 
crude flows overall remain resilient. Other regional producers 
have helped sustain exports, containing the immediate supply 
impact. However, adequate US commercial crude stocks 
mask thinner buffers elsewhere, with strategic reserves and 
fuel inventories low. This leaves the m arket more vulnerable 
to a prolonged disruption, supporting a higher geopolitical risk 
premium and an upward revision to our outlook.

15 Gold, crude oil – at a glance

Anthony Naab, CFA

### Page 16

Global Market Outlook 16

USD view

Further Fed tightening supports the USD near term. We expect the USD to remain supported, with our 3-month DXY forecast 
at 100.2. Resilient US payrolls for August alongside sticky energy and IT price -driven inflation reinforce the Fed's focus on 
containing inflation. Following its 25bp hike to 3.75–4.00%, we expect another hike by end-2026 and potentially another one in 
early H1 2027. Further tightening should keep US front -end yields elevated and support the USD. However, money markets 
already price a more aggressive Fed hiking path than our expectations, limiting the scope for further USD gains and leaving the 
USD vulnerable if incoming data weaken the case for additional hikes. 
Fading inflation and narrowing rate advantage point to a softer USD in the medium term. We expect the USD index (DXY) 
to decline gradually towards 98 over 12 months. Inflation pressures are expected to moderate  as the impact of oil prices and 
tariffs fade, reducing the need for continued Fed tightening. We forecast that the Fed funds rate may peak in H1 2027  in the 
current mini-rate hike cycle and then gradually decline thereafter as inflation moves closer towards target , supporting our DXY 
call. Further ECB and BoJ tightening should also progressively narrow the USD's relative rate advantage, supporting gradual 
USD moderation. Resilient US growth and elevated government bond yields should, however, limit the pace of depreciation.

EUR/USD 1.1380 1.15 1.18 
Near-term EUR weakness; constructive medium term. The ECB raised its deposit 
rate 25bps to 2.50% in September. Potential ECB tightening should narrow the US-
Euro area rate differential over time, supporting EUR over 12 months.

GBP/USD 1.3219 1.33 1.35 
Rangebound with near term downside risk: UK inflation rose to 3.1% y/y, keeping 
BoE tightening risk alive, but softer labour conditions should limit the extent of 
tightening. Fed -BoE policy divergence may constrain GBP near term, while an 
expected peak in US rates by mid -2027 should allow a modes t recovery over the 
medium term. The Autum Budget in October could increase volatility.

USD/JPY 158.86 157 153 
Constructive JPY. The BoJ's September 25bp hike to 1.25% reinforces policy 
normalisation. The still wide  US-Japan yield differential may limit near-term JPY 
gains, but further BoJ tightening should gradually narrow the gap and support JPY 
over the medium term towards our target of 153.

AUD/USD 0.7012 0.73 0.74 
Near-term AUD weakness; constructive medium term: Australia’s Q2 GDP grew 
0.4% q/q, reinforcing domestic resilience. A relatively restrictive RBA stance supports 
AUD, while eventual narrowing in relative rate differentials should improve the 
medium-term outlook.

NZD/USD 0.5662 0.56 0.57 
Rangebound: The RBNZ raised rates 25bps to 2.75%, while New Zealand’s Q2 
GDP grew 0.2% q/q. Improving growth provides support, but an uneven recovery and 
near-term USD strength should limit NZD gains.

USD/CAD 1.4138 1.39 1.37 
Near-term CAD weakness; modest medium-term recovery: Canada employment 
fell 41,700 in August vs. a 15,000 expected rise, while the BoC held rates at 2.25%. 
Softer labour conditions and Fed-BoC divergence may constrain CAD near term.

USD/CHF 0.8278 0.82 0.79 
Near-term CHF weakness; medium-term CHF recovery: Swiss inflation surprised 
higher at 0.8% y/y, but the wide Fed -SNB rate differential should continue to favour 
USD near term. As Fed tightening peaks and the US rate advantage narrows, CHF 
should gradually recover over 12 months.

USD/CNH 6.7159 6.70 6.67 
Gradual CNH appreciation: China’s industrial output in August rose 5.2% y/y, 
beating estimates, but retail sales growth slowed to 0.4% on weak domestic demand. 
Production resilience and PBoC currency support favour modest CNH appreciation, 
while soft consumption and an easing policy bias should limit the pace of gains.

USD/SGD 1.2794 1.28 1.26 
Rangebound near term; constructive SGD medium term: Further Fed tightening 
and elevated US yields may limit SGD appreciation near term. Resilient 
fundamentals and MAS's exchange -rate policy should support gradual SGD 
appreciation as US rate support fades.

USD/INR 95.96 – 98 
Modestly bearish INR: India’s crude oil imports rose 26% y/y in August, and a USD 
77bn AI import deficit are both adding to structural import pressures. Higher -for-
longer US rates remain a headwind, while capital inflows and RBI intervention should 
smooth out near-term volatility and support a gradual INR depreciation towards 98.

USD/MYR 4.0865 – 4.00 Modestly bullish MYR: Resilient domestic demand, strong exports and corporate FX conversion support MYR; external uncertainty should keep appreciation gradual.

USD/KRW 1,368.9 – 1,540 
Bearish KRW: Recent KRW -supportive corporate USD selling is fading, while 
reduced National Pension Service’s FX hedging and prospect of USD 350bn US 
investment package by the South Korean national investment committee could 
increase USD demand. Higher US rates add a he adwind, supporting KRW 
depreciation over 12 months.

### Page 18

Global Market Outlook 18

Summary

Our stock-bond model (3-6 months) reduced its Overweight allocation to global equities to +1 from +3 (maximum +5) 
in September. The downgrade was driven primarily by a weaker valuation signal as valuation score fell to -2 for both Developed 
Market and Asian equities. Despite some moderation, fundamentals remain strongly supportive of equities. Positive factors 
include resilient net earnings upgrades, a positive economic surprise index, and PMI new orders remaining above 50, signalling 
continued expansion in industrial activity. Market technicals weakened but continued to support equities overall. Our market 
breadth indicator shows that 72% of equity markets remain above their 200 -day moving averages. Stock net advances are 
positive and not at levels that would typically signal an imminent market reversal. The stock-bond model continues to outperform 
strongly, generating 4.2% alpha YTD relative to the 60/40 equity-bond benchmark. 
Our 1–3 month equity market regime models continue to signal low bear market risk, with probabilities of just 0.7% for 
S&P 500 and 5.0% for MSCI AC World  indices. The low readings continued to be driven by subdued option -market implied 
volatility. At 14 – 15%, the VIX and market cap -weighted one-month implied volatility measures for Europe and US remain 
comfortably below the 20% threshold at which bear market risk tends to rise sharply. While momentum indicators have softened, 
they point to a typical market consolidation rather than signs of meaningful market stress.  
Our positioning indicators are not signalling any reversal risk. Previous long positioning in DXY has fully unwound and our 
market diversity indicator for MSCI Singapore has also normalized after the index corrected from its September peak.

15 Quant perspective: Reducing Equity 
Overweight as Valuation Weakened

Francis Lim Senior Quantitative Strategist Maggie, Au Yeung Quantitative Analyst

### Page 19

Global Market Outlook 19 
Fig. 32 Long- and short-term quantitative models remain bullish on risk assets 
Long-term models below have a typical time horizon of 3-6 months, while short-term models have a 1-3-month horizon

Long-term Stock or bond US recession model 
Current view Reduce equity Overweight 20% probability within the next 6 months 
What factors 
is this view 
based on? 
• Fundamental: +2. 3-month change in PMI new 
orders indicate a slower pace in the expansion of 
industrial activity. All other factors such as net 
earnings upgrades, economic surprise index and 
risk sentiment remain positive.  
• Valuation: -2. Both DM and Asian equities are 
expensive as their valuation scores exceeded 1 
standard deviation of historical average.  
• Technical: +1. Net-advances in global stocks are 
positive but not extreme. Market breadth declined 
as equity markets above their 200-day moving 
averages fell from 85% to 72%, but it remains 
above the model’s 60% threshold. 
Estimated probability hovers around 20 – 21% 
• Conference Board consumer confidence 
remains weak at -8.6% y/y. 
• PMI new orders-to-inventory ratio is at 1.1x, 
below its historical average of 1.2x.  
• Conference Board leading indicators 
contracted by -1.1% y/y.  
• Fed funds rate-to-natural rate ratio is still high 
at 1.6x, reflecting a restrictive monetary policy 
compared to historical average of 1.3x. 
Key model 
factors 
• Economic activity, macro risk and surprise indices, 
corporate earnings, forward P/E ratio and technical 
factors. 
• Conference Board leading indicator, Consumer 
Confidence Index, ISM New Orders and Fed 
funds rate. 
How does it 
work? 
• A monthly scorecard of -5 to 5 based on 
fundamentals, valuations and market breadth 
factors to indicate the relative preference for bonds 
and equities. A positive score favours equities and 
vice versa. 
• A probability model based on regression. Long-
term statistical tests are conducted to identify 
the four key economic factors above.

Short-term Technical analysis Investor positioning 
Current views Bullish S&P 500 & MSCI AC World indices None flagged 
What factors 
is this view 
based on? 
• Bullish S&P500 and MSCI AC World. The 
probabilities of bear market sit at just 0.7% and 
5.0%, respectively, driven by subdued options-
market implied volatility. Momentum across both 
indices have weakened but they point to a typical 
market consolidation rather than signs of meaningful  
market stress.  
• Previous long positioning in the USD index 
(DXY) has fully unwound after DXY fell 
steeply form its peak in late July. The renewed 
strength in DXY since the Fed’s 16 Sept rate 
hike has started from neutral positioning.  
• Market diversity indicator for MSCI 
Singapore has normalised after the index 
corrected from its September peak. 
Key model 
factors 
• Market factors: Momentum, volatility, interest rate 
differentials, relative returns, inflation swap rates, 
economic surprises, etc. 
• Price action: Overbought conditions occur 
when prices rise sharply; oversold conditions 
happen when prices fall rapidly in a short time.  
How does it 
work? 
• Scanning through 7,000+ factors, the framework 
uses Machine Learning to forecast market regimes 
or future trends based on identified market drivers. 
• A market indicator based on fractal analysis 
that provides a timely indication of investor 
positioning based on price actions.

Source: Standard Chartered

### Page 24

Global Market Outlook 24

Time is your most precious commodity – be sure to spend it wisely 
Time is undoubtedly valuable. The days may seem long, but the years are short. So, make the choice 
to spend your time wisely. Whether you’re setting out on your investment journey, navigating the 
intricacies of mid -life wealth planning or fortifying assets  for your golden years, invest time today to 
ensure your wealth strategy is aligned to what’s right for you – Today, Tomorrow and Forever. 
Setting aside the time now to review your plan will pay dividends in the future. Markets have moved. 
Your portfolio’s current asset allocation may no longer be optimally positioned to maximise the 
opportunities ahead. Ask yourself the following: Am I holding too much cash? Am I sufficiently allocating 
to growth assets for the long term? Is my portfolio diversified? Am I capturing the best opportunities? 
And most importantly, is my wealth working hard for me so that I don’t have to? 
Use our SC Wealth Select framework and advisory specialists to help guide you through this process.

Purpose 
Today,  
Tomorrow,  
Forever 
Our approach to helping you grow and manage 
your wealth starts with you. We use a goals -
aware approach to understand your vision of 
Today, Tomorrow and Forever for yourself, your 
family and beyond, and then design portfolios to 
meet your various needs.  
Using our ‘Today, Tomorrow and Forever’ 
approach, we ensure your wealth needs for the 
near term (Today) are met, while ensuring your 
wealth needs for the decades ahead (Tomorrow 
and Forever) are also planned for.  
Your vision of ‘Today, Tomorrow and Forever’ is 
unique to you. Our specialists partner with you to 
build well -diversified, long -term Foundation 
portfolios, aligned to your Today, Tomorrow and 
Forever needs. Opportunistic ideas are added to 
capture short -term opportunities, a nd sufficient 
protection is included to address the objectives of 
you and your family. 
Today, Tomorrow and Forever 
Approach 
Planning for Today 
Requires ensuring liquidity and income 
flows take centre stage 
Securing Tomorrow 
Entails a well-diversified investment and 
protection portfolio with a focus on growth, 
ensuring inflation is accounted for and risks 
are mitigated 
Building for Forever 
Involves greater focus on long-term returns 
given the time horizon of your portfolio can 
be measured in decades, and might also 
include business interests, real estate, 
collectibles or charitable funds

SC Wealth Select  
 Managing your wealth through the decades – Today, Tomorrow and Forever

### Page 25

Global Market Outlook 25

Principles 
that stand the 
test of time 
Adhering to time-tested principles, to ensure your 
investment decisions remain robust and 
consistently applied, is paramount to your 
success Today, Tomorrow and Forever. We use 
five Wealth Principles to guide and guardrail your 
wealth decisions.

Discipline – ensure consistency and 
prudence over your emotions 
• Reacting to emotions, such as optimism and 
fear, can lead to poor investment decisions at 
the worst times 
• Have a plan and stick to it – this helps you to 
stay focused on the bigger picture

Diversification – simply put, don’t put 
all your eggs in one basket 
• Reduce risk by holding a variety of financial 
assets. Multi -asset diversification in your 
Foundation portfolio is important 
• As a guide, make sure your portfolio contains 
a variety of asset classes and investments 
that have low correlation with one another

Time in the Market – a more robust 
strategy than timing the market

• Predicting market selloffs is challenging, and 
timing your exit and re-entry is difficult 
• Missing out on the best performing days of a 
market can have a significantly detrimental 
impact on your portfolio 
• ‘Time in the market’ and buying the market 
with a longer -term view provide more 
consistent returns that can ride out bumps 
along the way

Risk and Return – make sure the risk 
is worth the return 
• To achieve higher investment returns, you will 
likely have to accept a greater level of risk in 
your portfolio 
• Therefore, it’s important to understand the 
risks and manage these on an ongoing basis

Protection – don’t let the unexpected 
catch you unprepared 
• Even though you may feel healthy, or 
financially stable now, protection offers the 
ability to overcome times of financial 
uncertainty and mitigate the long-term impact 
of unforeseen events on your wealth 
• A good protection plan not only safeguards 
your wealth today, but also considers the 
value of your future earnings over your 
lifetime, in today’s terms

Advisory 
Process 
Following a 
holistic 
approach to 
managing  
your wealth 
We follow a rigorous process to ensure your 
needs and objectives are well -understood, and 
your portfolio is well-aligned and manage s to  
deliver on these objectives. 
However, markets constantly evolve and your 
needs change. Hence, we encourage you to 
undertake regular portfolio reviews to ensure your 
portfolio remains aligned to your Today, 
Tomorrow and Forever objectives. This proactive 
approach includes strategic reb alancing based 
on insights from our Chief Investment Office.

Recent concerns have pushed France’s 10yr spread over Germany’s, to its highest since 2012
and the Euro crisis. But in an historical perspective, today’s spreads are only high by the
standards of the Euro Area when the two shared a common currency. They were regularly
several percentage points apart in the 70s and 80s.

Although much of the recent selloff is an unwinding of carry trades, France has ongoing, if not
new, fundamental issues. It hasn’t run a budget surplus since 1974, and the deficit was last
beneath 2% of GDP in 2001.

France has also seen its debt rise in relative terms. As recently as the GFC in 2008, France’s
debt-to-GDP was basically in line with Germany’s and well below Italy’s, but today it’s far
above Germany’s and closing in on Italy’s. Outside of the temporary Covid spike, Italian
debt/GDP has been stable for over a decade.

For most of the last decade, nominal GDP has been above 10yr yields, but that has reversed,
which is challenging from a debt sustainability if sustained – especially when debt/GDP is
pushing up against 120%. Recent inflation will slowly push up nominal GDP but the
relationship between the two will be important going forward.

long-term charts look worrying, but much of the sell-off in recent days/weeks has been due to a
clearing-out of carry trades, implemented assuming a much lower vol period than we’ve seen.

Document 380: Quantum dictionary, part 1_ Decoding the fundamentals From superposition to decoherence, we unpack the concepts and technologies shaping the future of quantum computing. 2 min read

Source PDF: all report pdf/Quantum dictionary, part 1_ Decoding the fundamentals From superposition to decoherence, we unpack the concepts and technologies shaping the future of quantum computing. 2 min read.pdf
Report source: Bank of America
Date: 2026-10-01
Pages: 9
Status: ok

### Page 1

13030420     1

Transformation 
Quantum dictionary, part 1: Decoding the fundamentals 
21 October 2026

Key takeaways 
• What is quantum computing? How does it work? What is a qubit? And when will quantum reach its full potential? Quantum 
computing challenges our intuition and our understanding of what computers can do. In this two-part dictionary series, we 
decode key terms behind what could be the next major technological revolution. 
• Qubits are the basic building blocks of quantum computers, harnessing properties such as superposition and entanglement to 
process information. However, qubits are highly sensitive to their surroundings where disturbances can lead to errors in 
quantum computation, making error correction a key challenge for building quantum computers. 
• There is no single approach to building a quantum computer. Researchers and companies are pursuing a range of qubit 
technologies, from neutral atoms and photons to spin, superconducting, topological and trapped-ion qubits, while quantum 
computing itself is likely to increasingly work alongside classical computing and AI.

First, let’s lay the groundwork… 
Quantum computing  
Quantum computing uses qubits and quantum properties such as superposition (defined later on) to perform complex 
calculations and solve certain problems faster than classical computers.1 From a practical perspective, quantum computing could 
be used to tackle problems related to optimization, machine learning, simulation and factorization across a range of sectors, 
including autos and logistics, financial services, energy and climate, health and life sciences and national security. 
Current quantum computers can perform ‘just’ thousands of quantum operations. As the technology advances from thousands to 
millions, billions and eventually trillions of operations, quantum computers could begin to have commercial applications.  
• Millions: Financial services and security could experience some of the first use cases, such as threat detection, fraud 
prevention, derivatives pricing, bond trading and arbitrage. 
• Billions: Quantum could help power AI through advanced data generation capabilities.  
• Trillions: Quantum chemistry applications could emerge, including drug discovery, battery simulation and materials 
discovery. 
These are the publicly known applications, according to Oxford Quantum Circuits (OQC). And as quantum computing capabilities 
improve, some of these use cases could emerge sooner than anticipated.  
Now, we’ll tackle the core concepts… 
Qubits 
Qubits (quantum bits) are a quantum computer’s basic unit of information. They can exhibit quantum properties such as 
superposition and entanglement (which we elaborate on below) — properties fundamental to quantum computing. 
Quantum gates manipulate the state of qubits, much like logic gates manipulate bits in classical computers. Despite the name, a 
quantum gate is not a physical object, but rather an operation applied to a quantum system that changes its state. 
From physical to logical qubits 
Physical qubits are the foundation of a quantum computer’s hardware. However, they are highly susceptible to error. To address 
this, multiple physical qubits can be structured together to encode a logical qubit, allowing errors to be detected and corrected

1 Schneider, J., & Smalley, I. (2025, June 10). What is quantum computing? IBM. 
I NSTI TUTE
Accessible version

### Page 2

2 21 October 2026 
  I NSTI TUTE

and making the stored quantum information more reliable. In recent years, according to BofA Global Research, logical qubits 
have become a greater focus for quantum computing companies. 
Entanglement 
Entanglement is a condition where two or more qubits become linked so that their states are correlated, regardless of distance 
(Exhibit 1). It allows quantum computers to process information in ways that can be difficult or impossible for classical 
computers. 
So, how does it work? Suppose we have two photons that have opposite polarizations (the orientation of their light waves). 
Before measurement, neither has a definite polarization. When one is measured as horizontally polarized, the other will be 
vertically polarized when measured in the same basis, wherever it is. When measured separately, they appear to be random, but 
when compared, they are correlated (Exhibit 2). 
The challenges 
Once created, entanglement must be protected from decoherence (where interactions with the environment destroy quantum 
correlations). Maintaining entanglement over long distances is another challenge. Photons traveling through optical fiber can be 
absorbed or scattered, making long-distance quantum communication difficult. 2 Quantum repeaters aim to overcome this 
challenge by extending entanglement across a series of shorter links. 
Exhibit 1: Entanglement is a state where at least two qubits become 
correlated 
An infographic of entangled qubits

Source: BofA Global Research 
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE

Exhibit 2: Measuring one qubit in an entangled pair instantly 
determines the state of the other, no matter the distance 
An example of what entanglement means for qubit measurement

Source: BofA Global Research 
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE

Superposition 
Unlike a classical bit, which can be one of two states (0 or 1), superposition allows a quantum system to exist in multiple states 
or configurations at the same time (Exhibit 3). For example, a qubit can exist in the 0 state, the 1 state or any state that is a 
combination of 0 and 1.3  
A quantum system can maintain a superposition until it is observed or measured. Once measured, its superposition collapses into 
a single state. Another way of thinking about superposition is to imagine a sphere with the north pole representing zero and the 
south pole representing one. Other points on the surface of the sphere represent different superposition states. This allows us 
to: 1) represent complex forms of information; and 2) simulate atoms in a computer. 
Schrödinger’s cat 
In 1935, physicist Erwin Schrödinger proposed a thought experiment to illustrate the strange implications of quantum 
mechanics. In this scenario, a cat, a radioactive substance that decays very slowly, and a Geiger counter are put together in a 
sealed box. According to quantum mechanics, the system can be described as a superposition in which the cat is both alive and 
dead until the box is opened. Once the box is opened and the system is observed, the superposition collapses into a single 
outcome.

2 Rehman, M. (2026, May 11). What is Quantum Entanglement? Quantum Insider. 
3 Microsoft. (n.d.). Superposition.

### Page 3

21 October 2026    3

I NSTI TUTE

Enabling parallel computation versus classical computing 
Superposition is a key attribute of quantum computing that allows for quantum parallelism, or the ability to perform multiple 
computations at the same time. Classical bits can only be in one of two states, 0 or 1. In contrast, a qubit can be in a 
superposition of 0 and 1, allowing a quantum computer to process multiple possible qubit states at once.4  
Exhibit 3: Superposition is the condition where a qubit can exist in a superposition of 0 and 1 
Illustrating the power of superposition

Source: BofA Global Research 
Note: A Bloch sphere is a geometric representation of qubit states. 
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
Error rates and fidelity 
Fidelity is a measure of how closely a quantum state or operation matches what was intended (Exhibit 4). A higher fidelity 
generally means fewer errors. 
Physical qubits are prone to errors. One way to address this is to combine multiple physical qubits to create a more reliable 
logical qubit. Quantum error correction (QEC) allows errors in the physical qubits to be detected and corrected, helping protect 
the information stored in the logical qubit.

4 Ibid.

### Page 4

4 21 October 2026 
  I NSTI TUTE

Exhibit 4: Fidelity measures how accurately a qubit retains its intended quantum state during computation 
Infographic showing the implications of high versus low fidelity

Source: BofA Global Research  
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
How does quantum error correction work? 
At its simplest, QEC relies on redundancy, or storing information across multiple qubits so that errors can be detected and 
corrected.  
Consider an example: suppose someone wants to send a message represented by 0. Instead of sending a single 0, the message 
could be repeated three times as 000, known as a codeword. If one bit were accidentally flipped from 0 to 1 during transmission, 
the recipient might receive 010. Because two of the three bits are still 0, the recipient could infer that the intended message 
was 000. 
However, if two bits flipped, producing 110 or 011, the recipient could infer the wrong message. If all three flipped to 111, the 
recipient would have no way to know from the received codeword that errors had occurred.5 This approach is known as a 
repetition code and illustrates how redundancy can help protect information from errors.  
Repetition codes are a simple form of error correction that can address bit-flip errors, where a 0 changes to a 1 or vice versa. 
More sophisticated QEC codes are needed to address other quantum errors, such as phase flips.6 Shor’s code, developed by 
American theoretical computer scientist Peter Shor, uses nine physical qubits to encode one logical qubit and can correct one 
bit-flip error or one phase flip error, but it can’t correct both types of errors at the same time. Meanwhile, Steane’s seven-qubit 
code, developed by Oxford physicist Andrew Steane, can also correct both types of errors. It has the advantage of being fault-
tolerant, meaning the error-correction process itself does not introduce additional errors.7 
At what error level might we start seeing quantum advantage? 
Some classical computing systems today operate with extremely high reliability, often described as having "17 nines" of fidelity 
(99.99999999999999999%). By comparison, quantum computers currently achieve fidelity rates of around 99.9%. Gerald 
Mullally, CEO of OQC, has argued that quantum systems would need to add roughly three more nines of fidelity to achieve 
commercial relevance, or quantum advantage. 
Noise and decoherence 
Noise refers to unwanted disturbances that affect quantum systems and can lead to errors in computations (Exhibit 5). It can 
come from sources such as interactions with the environment, electromagnetic interference and temperature fluctuations. 
Coherence is a quantum system’s ability to maintain its quantum properties, including superposition and entanglement. When 
coherence is lost through interactions with the environment, the process is known as “decoherence,” causing the system to 
behave more classically (Exhibit 6).8

5 Microsoft Ignite. (n.d.) Introduction to quantum error correction. 
6 Ibid. 
7 Ibid. 
8 Quandela. (n.d.) Quantum Decoherence.

### Page 5

21 October 2026    5

I NSTI TUTE

To fully control qubits for calculations and information transfer, they need to be carefully protected from their surroundings. 
External factors like noise, energy, changes in Earth’s magnetic field, radiation from Wi-Fi or mobile phones or cosmic rays can 
interfere with the process. Even small disturbances can lead to decoherence, making it difficult to develop large-scale, fault-
tolerant quantum computers.9 
According to QuEra Computing, quantum noise can be mitigated through physical isolation of qubits, the development of more 
precise control techniques and quantum error correction, which helps detect and correct errors in physical qubits.  
Exhibit 5: Noise refers to unwanted disturbances, e.g., interactions 
with the environment, that lead to errors in quantum computation 
An infographic explaining quantum noise

Source: BofA Global Research 
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE

Exhibit 6: Decoherence is when qubits lose their key quantum 
properties, superposition and entanglement 
An infographic of decoherence

Source: BofA Global Research 
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE

Quantum processing unit (QPU) 
Referred to as the brain of the quantum computer, the QPU is the core hardware where qubits reside and where quantum 
computations take place. QPUs rely on quantum behaviors like superposition and entanglement to tackle problems in areas such 
as cryptography, simulation and optimization. Just as a CPU (central processing unit) in classical computing contains multiple 
components, a QPU includes quantum gates (operations that manipulate qubits in a controlled way, similar to logic gates in 
classical computing) and interconnects, which enable communication between qubits and other quantum processors.10 
Next, we’ll look at broader computing architecture… 
Heterogeneous computing  
Heterogeneous computing refers to a future computing landscape likely combining classical and quantum computing, potentially 
unlocking more powerful, faster and cheaper compute and helping solve problems that classical computers cannot. According to 
BofA Global Research, AI and quantum computing could also converge and complement each other, enabling more 
transformative use cases. 
Quantum modality  
A quantum modality refers to the type of physical technology used to create and manipulate qubits. We discuss the modalities in 
greater depth in the following section. 
Lastly, we outline qubit technologies… 
Neutral atom qubit 
A quantum modality that uses individual atoms with no electrical charge as qubits (Exhibit 7). The atoms are held in place using 
focused lasers known as optical tweezers. Neutral atom qubit systems do not require atoms to be ionized, which means fewer 
energy constraints and potentially simpler cooling requirements. Atoms such as rubidium or cesium are typically used. The qubits 
are manipulated by changing the energy levels or positions of the trapped atoms using laser pulses.

9 QuEra. (n.d.) Quantum Noise. 
10 SpinQ. (2025, January 16). What does a Quantum Computer Look Like? Explain Key Parts.

### Page 6

6 21 October 2026 
  I NSTI TUTE

Exhibit 7: A quantum modality where atoms with a net electrical charge of zero are the foundation for qubits 
Key features of neutral atoms

Source: BofA Global Research 
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
Photonic qubit 
A quantum modality that uses particles of light, known as photons, to carry quantum information (Exhibit 8).11 Optical 
components such as beam splitters, which divide light along different paths, and phase shifters, which change the phase of light, 
can be used to perform quantum operations. Photonic quantum computers can use integrated photonic chips to manipulate 
photons and enable quantum logic. 
Exhibit 8: A quantum modality relying on the quantum properties of light 
Key features of photonic qubits

Source: BofA Global Research 
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE

11 Moore, J. (2025, March 28). The 6 different types of quantum computing technology. TechTarget.

### Page 7

21 October 2026    7

I NSTI TUTE

Spin qubit 
A quantum modality that uses the intrinsic spin of electrons or nuclei in solid-state materials, such as silicon or diamond. These 
spins can be manipulated using magnetic or electric fields, and their states are read using optical or charge-sensing techniques. 
Spin qubits are particularly appealing because they can leverage existing CMOS (complementary metal-oxide semiconductor; a 
technology to build processors) manufacturing infrastructure, making it easier to integrate them with conventional processors. 
Superconducting qubit 
A quantum modality in which electronic circuits create qubits. They are built using Josephson junctions, where two 
superconductors are separated by a thin insulating layer. Microwave pulses manipulate the qubits’ quantum states to run 
computations.12  
Specifically, a resistance-free current oscillates back and forth around a quantum loop. An injected microwave signal excites the 
current into superposition states.13 To enable this process, superconducting materials are cooled to near absolute zero (around 
10-20 millikelvin) using dilution refrigerators, helping reduce thermal noise and maintain coherence. 
Topological qubit 
A quantum modality that aims to encode quantum information using topological properties rather than relying only on individual 
particle states (Exhibit 9). One way to think about this is through the paths certain quasiparticles take as they move around one 
another, creating “braids” in space-time. Because the information can depend on the overall pattern of these braids rather than 
small details of their paths, the approach is designed to be more resilient to certain disturbances. 
Exhibit 9: Resilient to small perturbations but requires special quasiparticles not yet been conclusively demonstrated in nature 
An infographic of topological qubits

Source: BofA Global Research 
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
Trapped-ion qubit  
A quantum modality in which ions (electrically charged atoms) are held in place using electromagnetic fields. These ions act as 
the qubits and are controlled by the system with tightly focused laser beams.14 The qubits are held inside ultra-high vacuum 
(UHV) chambers.

12 Ibid.

### Page 1

Taiwan Earnings Call Weekly Recap - Is Earnings
Still Enough to Drive Stock Price? | GSTW
30 October 2026 | 4:17 PM Taiwan Standard Time
Robert Wang
Goldman Sachs (Asia) L.L.C. Taipei Branch
FICC & Equities
Short week but busy for Taiwan in terms of earnings. Index is only down 1.8% WTD,
though multiple large caps saw over 10% drawdown in this week alone. The elevated
level in Taiwan vs other regional markets still attracted much short interest without

much buying interest except from retail. Overall sentiment is not bearish, though
investors have turned much more defensive and hiding in sectors such as Financials,
Telecom, and TSMC.
For earnings, winners' momentum was short lived; instead of chasing into the beat,
many chose to further profit take on the strength (Unimicron, EMC/TUC, Landmark
on open right after their earnings). Companies that missed the results were definitely
punished (i.e. Chroma, Delta, UMC). Echoing back to my previous note, range bound

trade seems to be the safest way to stay afloat.
See below for my single stock takeaway:
Mediatek (2454 TT)
Financial Highlights:
3Q26 revenue reached NT$168.06bn (+10% QoQ and +18% YoY) with EPS at
NT$17.5 (+15% QoQ and +11% YoY) vs cons. NT$15.95. GM came in at 46.3%
(+11ppt QoQ and 18ppt YoY) vs cons. 46.2%.
3Q26 outperformance was mainly from stronger-than-expected customer
demand and improved supply chain yield. First ASIC already started shipping since

Sep'26.
Operational Outlook:
Revenue outlook for 4Q26 is NT$210 - NT$222bn, translating to a 29% sequential
growth. 2027 revenue growth will likely grow by high single digits yoy. GM will be
around 45% with +/-1.5%, and OPM is expected to be at 26% +/-2%.
ASIC chip sales for 2026 will be around US$2.5bn, higher than previous guidance,
and it will reach US$16bn in 2027, around 20% of ASIC TAM and at the higher end
of the company guidance. Next gen chip's tape out is on track and targeted to

enter mass production in 1Q28.
Delta Electronics (2308 TT)
10/30/26, 4:46 PM Taiwan Earnings Call Weekly Recap - Is Earnings Still Enough to Drive Stock Price? | GSTW
https://marquee.gs.com/s/content/markets/2971c821-c2cc-403a-acfe-2b906dd125c4 1/11

### Page 2

Financial Highlights:
3Q26 revenue reached NT$197.1bn (+8% QoQ / +31% YoY), while EPS registered
at NT$9.05. GM declined to 34.1% (down 1.5ppt QoQ), impacted by a NT$3.9bn
inventory write-down (~1.7% of revenue) mostly in Power Electronics.
Operating profit rose +7% QoQ (+31% YoY) backed by operating leverage, keeping
OPM resilient at 16.6% flat QoQ. Net profit was NT$23.5bn (-7% QoQ / +26% YoY)
due to lower non-operating investment gains.
Operational Outlook:

Management expects 4Q26 revenue to grow sequentially with gross margins
unlikely to deteriorate, aided by inventory normalization and potential price
adjustments to offset ~2% YTD material cost inflation.
AI-related exposure approaches 50% of total revenue (liquid cooling at ~13%), and
management remains confident in AI infrastructure demand through 2030 despite
near-term bottlenecks.
Key Q&A:
Inventory Write-down & Normalization: The NT$3.9bn write-down stemmed from

component mismatches and slower customer ramp-ups of newer platforms (e.g.,
18.5kW vs. 5.5kW platforms); management expects affected inventory to be
progressively consumed by 1Q27.
HVDC Adoption & Vicor Litigation: While 400V products saw partial shipments in
3Q26, 800V adoption is slower and primarily NVIDIA-driven. On the Vicor VPD
patent dispute, management expects no near-term impact on customer
relationships or shipments, with an initial ITC determination unlikely until 2027.

ASE Technology Holding (3711 TT)
Financial Highlights:
3Q26 consolidated revenue reached NT$244.16bn, with a GM of 22.1% and EPS of
NT$5.84, all outperforming market expectations.
ATM (Advanced Semiconductor Engineering, Test and Materials) GM hit 30.6%,
breaking past previous structural highs; the company announced 30% as its
baseline gross margin for the future.
Operational Outlook:
4Q26 consolidated revenue is projected to grow 6%–8% QoQ, with GM between

23.0%–24.0% and OPM between 14.5%–15.5%.
CPO is set to enter mass production in 4Q26, with related revenue expected to
contribute over US$500mn in 2027; the full-year LEAP revenue target has been
revised upward to over US$4bn.
Key Q&A:
Capex Upward Revision: Due to strong demand for LEAP and mainstream
advanced packaging, full-year 2026 capex was raised from US$10.5bn to
US$11.5bn (60% allocated to LEAP, 40% to mainstream packaging), with 2027
capex expected to be even higher.

Capacity Bottlenecks & Expansion: Factory floor space is currently the primary
constraint on near-term growth. The company is actively searching for and
acquiring existing facilities to shorten setup timelines, prioritizing resource
allocation toward packaging and wafer sort.
Elite Material (2383 TT)
10/30/26, 4:46 PM Taiwan Earnings Call Weekly Recap - Is Earnings Still Enough to Drive Stock Price? | GSTW
https://marquee.gs.com/s/content/markets/2971c821-c2cc-403a-acfe-2b906dd125c4 2/11

### Page 3

Financial Highlights:
Driven by strong demand, actual 3Q26 revenue grew 29% QoQ, comfortably
beating prior conservative expectations.
GM outperformed expectations, hitting 37.7% for the quarter.
Operational Outlook:
4Q26 revenue growth is conservatively estimated to be at least 15%–20% QoQ,
with gross margins expanding further to 38.0%–39.5%.
Proactive capacity expansion plans (targeting 15.25 million sheets/month by end-
2030 vs. 5.85 million sheets/month currently) are still insufficient to support all

signed Long-Term Agreements (LTAs) for the next 4 years, pointing to a severe
supply-demand gap and zero oversupply risk.
Key Q&A:
CCL Pricing & Substrate Expansion: EMC is executing its second round of high-end
CCL (M7+) price hikes this year (5%–20% increases covering M7/M8 and M8+/M9
grades). Furthermore, it is rapidly expanding high-end ABF substrate core-layer
CCL capacity from 300k sheets/month to at least 1.2 million sheets/month by end-
2027, targeting commercial shipments starting in 4Q27.

Competition & Market Share: While AI customers are qualifying second or third
sources to secure supply chains, EMC maintains its long-term target of holding a
50% AI CCL market share, supported by best-in-class product quality and deep
CSP/PCB collaboration.
Yageo Corporation (2327 TT)
Financial Highlights:
3Q26 revenue reached NT$49.53bn (+11.4% QoQ, +49.7% YoY), with a GM of 41%
and EPS of NT$5.83.
Free cash flow surged to NT$9.236 billion, while the net financial debt-to-EBITDA

ratio dropped from 59% in 4Q25 to 24% in 3Q26, reflecting a continuously
improving financial structure and robust cash generation.
Operational Outlook:
4Q26 revenue, GM, and OPM are all expected to edge up sequentially, defying
typical seasonal flat-to-down trends for the fourth quarter.
AI demand remains solid, with its contribution to total revenue expanding from
15% in 1Q26 and 16% in 2Q26 to approximately 20% in 3Q26.
Key Q&A:
Capacity & Expansion Plans: Capacity utilization rates stood at 95% in 3Q26 and

are expected to exceed 95% in 4Q26, surpassing the healthy range of 85%–90%.
Total capacity for tantalum capacitors and MLCCs is planned to expand by 10%–
15% in 2027, with portions coming online starting in the second quarter.
Order Momentum & B/B Ratio: The overall book-to-bill (B/B) ratio in 3Q26
remained close to 2.0, with AI-related tantalum capacitors and MLCCs seeing
particularly robust orders, while LTAs with core customers continue to underpin

future shipments.
Nan Ya Plastics (1303 TT)
Financial Highlights:

https://marquee.gs.com/s/content/markets/2971c821-c2cc-403a-acfe-2b906dd125c4 3/11

### Page 4

3Q26 revenue rose to NT$95.6bn (+14.3% QoQ), with electronic materials
expanding to 58.1% of total sales. Operating profit surged 61.2% QoQ to
NT$18.31bn, of which electronic materials contributed 93.8%.
EPS hit a record NT$4.90, bringing 9M26 EPS to a record NT$10.07, supported by
electronic material profitability and investment income from Nanya Technology.
Operational Outlook:
CCL has surpassed NYPCB as the segment's primary revenue driver (31% of

electronic materials) with segment OPM scaling to 30.9% (up from 19.9% in 2Q26).
Management guided for fiberglass fabrics and CCL price hikes to continue at a 5–
10% MoM increase pace through early 2027, alongside a notable product mix shift
toward M7/M8 in 1Q27.
Key Q&A:
CCL Pricing and M-Series Mix: CCL and fiberglass-cloth pricing remains firm into
early 2027 with monthly 5–10% increases. M-series products reached ~30% of
CCL revenue in September, with management expecting further mix improvement

toward M7/M8 as CSP projects lock in.
Capacity Expansion and M10 Development: Monthly CCL capacity is projected to
expand from 6.18m to 6.48m sheets by year-end. For M9/M10, Nan Ya is
independently formulating hydrocarbon resins and fiberglass incorporating low-
dielectric and quartz materials with solid sampling progress.
TUC (6274 TT)
Financial Highlights:
3Q26 financial results beat market consensus, reporting revenue of NT17.95bn,
GM of 36.9%, operating margin of 31.0%, and EPS of NT$12.88, outperforming

consensus estimates by 29% to 35%.
The earnings beat was driven by continuous growth in high-end product revenue
and a return to profitability for the Thailand plant, which successfully offset earlier
first-half losses.
Operational Outlook:
For 4Q26, high-end product pricing is initiating upward adjustments. With the
Thailand plant adding another 100k sheets of capacity and all series of CCL seeing
price hike opportunities (especially high-end M7/M8 catch-up price increases

alongside tightening mid-to-low-end fiberglass cloth supply), product mix and
profitability are set to improve further.
ASIC AI servers are emerging as a core growth engine, alongside long-term
opportunities from satellite and space data centers. TUC is adding several large
ASIC customers (some already in sampling/board qualification stages) and
expanding its customer base in 400G, 800G, and 1.6T switches.
Key Q&A / Detailed Highlights:
ASIC & Switch Expansion: AI servers will serve as the primary growth driver, with

multiple major ASIC clients entering the board-maker certification stage;
meanwhile, existing 400G switch customers are rapidly accelerating 800G and 1.6T
switch certifications, with some expecting 1.6T demand to outpace 800G by 2027.
Satellite and PTFE Solutions: Space satellite PCBs utilizing M8+ materials are slated
to begin contribution in 1H27, with long-term customer demand forecasts already
extended through 2032. For PTFE, TUC boasts ~5–6 years of R&D foundation

rather than being a newcomer, though external partnerships may be pursued to
bypass special high-temperature press machine capacity limitations

Win Semiconductors (3105 TT)
Financial Highlights:
3Q26 revenue reached NT$5.86bn (+11.5% QoQ / +31% YoY) with capacity
utilization improving to 75%. GM expanded significantly by 9.2ppts QoQ to an 18-
quarter high of 37.4% (standalone GM reached 40.7%), while OPM rose to 24.2%
and EPS reached NT$4.55.
Margin expansion was primarily driven by a favorable product mix shift toward
higher-margin Infrastructure and Optical products rather than utilization gains

alone.
Operational Outlook:
Management guided 4Q26 revenue for low-single-digit QoQ growth—beating
normal smartphone seasonality—with gross margins sustaining in the high-30%
range.
Moving into FY27, photodetector (PD) production is expected to ramp materially,
while CW Laser and EML are anticipated to contribute more meaningfully starting
in FY28[cite: 388].
Key Q&A:
Infrastructure and Satellite Growth: Infrastructure became WIN's largest revenue

contributor at 35–40% in 3Q26, driven by LEO satellites, aerospace, and long-
distance communications; demand is expected to stay robust into FY27.
Optical and PD Production Timeline: Optical revenue rose QoQ on initial PD mass
production; PD will see significantly higher full-year shipment volumes in FY27,
while CW Laser and EML will generate limited initial revenue before a FY28 volume
ramp.

performance on that day of their underlying index or benchmark. For this reason, if they are held for a period longer than one day, their
performance over such longer periods of time can differ significantly from the stated multiple of the performance (or the inverse of the
performance) of their underlying index or benchmark during the same period of time. This effect can be magnified in volatile markets.
Prior to entering into a transaction in leveraged or inverse ETFs, you should be aware of the general risks associated with such

transactions. You should not enter into leveraged or inverse ETFs transactions unless you understand the nature and extent of your risk
exposure. You should also be satisfied that the leveraged or inverse ETFs transaction is appropriate for you in light of your circumstances
and financial condition. ETFs and ETNs that are linked to commodity futures do not offer direct exposure to the commodity's spot price,
and may perform differently than the spot price for the commodity itself. Performance differential can be magnified if a specific

condition persists in the market for a commodity that creates a disparity between near-term future prices and long-term future prices
and may lead to unexpected performance results. Other factors, such as roll yield, transaction costs, management fees, and taxes may
cause deviation in performance between the spot price of a commodity and commodity futures. You should not assume that an ETF or
ETN that is linked to commodity futures will provide an effective hedge because of a negative correlation with equities or other asset

swaps, and other derivatives as well as non-investment-grade securities - give rise to substantial risk and are not available to nor suitable
for all investors. If you have any questions about whether you are eligible to enter into these transactions with Goldman Sachs, please
contact your sales representative. Foreign-currency-denominated securities are subject to fluctuations in exchange rates that could have
an adverse effect on the value or price of, or income derived from, the investment. In addition, investors in securities such as ADRs, the

values of which are influenced by foreign currencies, effectively assume currency risk.

Document 539: 中国联通Q3营收同比微增0.5%，净利润下降31.3% _ 财报见闻

Source PDF: all report pdf/中国联通Q3营收同比微增0.5%，净利润下降31.3% _ 财报见闻.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-29
Pages: 4
Status: ok

### Page 1

三季度单季营业收⼊ 932.83 亿元，同⽐增⻓ 0.5%
摘要：
中国联通三季报呈现出较为典型的 “ 收⼊稳、利润压 ” 的特征。
2026 年前三季度，公司实现营业收⼊ 2946.47 亿元，同⽐增⻓ 0.6% ；第三季度单季营业收⼊
932.83 亿元，同⽐增⻓ 0.5% ，收⼊端基本保持平稳。
但利润端压⼒明显放⼤。前三季度，中国联通利润总额 161.02 亿元，同⽐下降 34.6% ；归⺟净利
润 58.03 亿元，同⽐下降 33.8% ；扣⾮归⺟净利润 48.66 亿元，同⽐下降 36.2% 。第三季度单季，
公司归⺟净利润 16.64 亿元，同⽐下降 31.3% ，扣⾮归⺟净利润 13.32 亿元，同⽐下降 34.8% 。
财报发布后，中国联通股价下跌，市场反应主要指向利润端的降幅明显⼤于收⼊端，以及成
本、费⽤和应收账款扩张带来的压⼒。不过，这份财报并⾮没有亮点：经营现⾦流仍保持增
⻓，新业务中算⼒、国际业务继续实现双位数增⻓，连接规模也在扩⼤。
经营层⾯，公司总连接数突破 13 亿，其中移动及宽带⽤⼾净增 1240 万⼾，物联⽹连接净增超

6600 万个；算⼒收⼊同⽐增⻓ 12.2% ，国际业务收⼊同⽐增⻓ 14.5% 。这意味着中国联通的转型
业务仍在扩张，只是短期尚未完全抵消传统业务收⼊低速增⻓和成本上⾏对利润的影响。
收⼊基本横盘，利润率明显承压
从收⼊看，中国联通前三季度营业收⼊ 2946.47 亿元，同⽐增加约 16.61 亿元，增幅仅 0.6% ；第
三季度单季收⼊同⽐增⻓ 0.5% ，延续低速增⻓状态。
相⽐之下，利润端下滑幅度显著更⼤。前三季度归⺟净利润 58.03 亿元，同⽐减少约 29.69 亿
元；扣⾮归⺟净利润 48.66 亿元，同⽐减少约 27.64 亿元。
利润率也同步⾛低。前三季度，公司利润总额率约为 5.5% ，⽽上年同期约为 8.4% ；归⺟净利率
约为 2.0% ，低于上年同期约 3.0% 的⽔平。第三季度单季归⺟净利率约为 1.8% ，扣⾮归⺟净利率
约为 1.4% 。
这也是市场对财报反应偏谨慎的重要原因：收⼊仍然稳定，但盈利弹性明显转弱。
成本增⻓快于收⼊，是利润下滑主线
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

利润承压的核⼼在成本端。
前三季度，中国联通营业总成本 2761.08 亿元，同⽐增⻓约 4.5% ，明显快于 0.6% 的收⼊增速。
其中，营业成本 2238.88 亿元，同⽐增加约 99.21 亿元，是拖累利润的最主要项⽬。
费⽤端表现分化：
销售费⽤ 268.78 亿元，同⽐下降约 1.8% ，有所压降；
管理费⽤ 180.03 亿元，同⽐增⻓约 11.4% ；
研发费⽤ 61.96 亿元，同⽐增⻓约 7.5% ；
税⾦及附加 16.76 亿元，同⽐增⻓约 28.1% 。
也就是说，公司在销售端有控制费⽤的动作，但⽹络运营、转型投⼊、管理⽀出和研发投⼊仍
推动整体成本率上⾏。
前三季度营业成本占收⼊⽐例约 76.0% ，⾼于上年同期约 73.0% ；营业总成本占收⼊⽐例约
93.7% ，也⾼于上年同期约 90.2% 。在收⼊⼏乎横盘的背景下，成本率的抬升直接压缩了利润空
间。
信⽤减值损失仍⾼，但较去年同期有所改善
财报中另⼀个值得关注的项⽬是信⽤减值损失。
前三季度，中国联通确认信⽤减值损失 90.08 亿元，虽然较上年同期的 109.30 亿元有所收窄，但

绝对⾦额仍然较⾼，对利润形成明显侵蚀。
同时，资产处置收益 14.59 亿元，低于上年同期 17.34 亿元；投资收益 38.67 亿元，⾼于上年同期
34.88 亿元，其中对联营企业和合营企业的投资收益 33.83 亿元。
⾮经常性损益⽅⾯，前三季度公司⾮经常性损益合计 9.37 亿元，主要来⾃⾮流动资产处置损益、
政府补助等。对应来看，⾮经常性损益约占归⺟净利润的 16% ，对利润有⼀定⽀撑，但扣⾮净
利润同⽐下降 36.2% ，说明主营经营利润压⼒更为直接。
算⼒、国际业务增⻓较快，转型业务仍是亮点
虽然利润端表现较弱，但中国联通在转型业务上的增⻓仍值得关注。
公司披露，前三季度算⼒收⼊同⽐增⻓ 12.2% ，其中智算中⼼收⼊同⽐增⻓ 10.4% ；国际业务收
⼊同⽐增⻓ 14.5% 。在运营商传统通信业务增⻓趋缓的⼤背景下，算⼒、云、 AI 、政企数字化和
国际化业务，是中国联通寻找新增量的主要⽅向。
连接规模也继续扩⼤。公司总连接数突破 13 亿，移动及宽带⽤⼾净增 1240 万⼾，物联⽹连接净

增超 6600 万个。⽹络资源⽅⾯， 4G/5G 基站总数超过 480 万座，万兆光⽹已在百余个城市商⽤试
点；标准机架达到 120 万架， IDC 资源总规模 3000MW 。
此外，公司云智产品付费⽤⼾突破 1.2 亿⼾，并称已打造万余家 5G ⼯⼚。研发⽅⾯，前三季度研
发费⽤同⽐增⻓ 7.5% ，公司还发布了包括联通 AIDN 创新架构、 6G 创新平台、联通云 8.0 、元景
MaaS 、联通墨攻 3.0 等在内的九项创新成果。

### Page 3

这些业务代表了中国联通向 “ 数智综合服务商 ” 转型的⽅向，但从本期财报看，转型业务的增⻓尚
不⾜以在短期完全对冲成本扩张和传统业务低增速带来的利润压⼒。
经营现⾦流保持增⻓，资本开⽀略有下降
现⾦流表现相对稳健。
前三季度，中国联通经营活动产⽣的现⾦流量净额 596.86 亿元，同⽐增⻓ 2.3% 。同期，公司销
售商品、提供劳务收到的现⾦ 2949.16 亿元，与营业收⼊基本匹配，显⽰经营现⾦回款总体保持
稳定。
资本开⽀相关现⾦流⽅⾯，前三季度购建固定资产、⽆形资产和其他⻓期资产⽀付现⾦ 425.83
亿元，较上年同期 433.21 亿元略有下降。以经营现⾦流扣除该项资本性⽀出粗略测算，公司前
三季度⾃由现⾦流约 171 亿元，⾼于上年同期约 150 亿元。
不过，投资活动现⾦流仍为⼤额净流出。前三季度投资活动现⾦流量净额为 -442.34 亿元，上年
同期为 -436.86 亿元。其中，投资⽀付现⾦ 491.82 亿元，明显⾼于上年同期 179.20 亿元；同时收
回投资收到现⾦ 281.58 亿元，也⾼于上年同期。

筹资活动现⾦流净额为 -112.31 亿元，较上年同期 -154.06 亿元有所收窄，主要与分红、偿债、租
赁等资⾦流出有关。
应收账款增⻓较快，合同负债下降
资产负债表中，最值得跟踪的是应收账款。
截⾄三季度末，中国联通应收账款 789.38 亿元，较去年末 647.57 亿元增加约 141.82 亿元，增幅
约 21.9% 。在同期营业收⼊仅增⻓ 0.6% 的情况下，应收账款扩张较快，可能反映部分政企、产
业互联⽹或项⽬型业务回款周期拉⻓。
同时，公司合同负债 421.75 亿元，较去年末 470.18 亿元减少约 48.43 亿元。合同负债下降通常意
味着预收款或未履约义务减少，后续需要关注其对收⼊确认和现⾦流的影响。
其他资产项⽬中，存货 49.79 亿元，较去年末 24.31 亿元翻倍；在建⼯程 359.08 亿元，较去年末
304.84 亿元增⻓约 17.8% ；固定资产 3018.83 亿元，较去年末 3141.23 亿元下降。整体看，公司
⽹络和算⼒基础设施仍处于持续投⼊和结构调整阶段。

负债端总体平稳。截⾄三季度末，公司总资产 6824.54 亿元，较去年末增⻓ 1.7% ；归⺟所有者权
益 1730.37 亿元，较去年末增⻓ 2.6% ；负债合计 2967.64 亿元，较去年末⼩幅增加。资产负债率
约 43.5% ，整体杠杆⽔平不⾼。
股价为何下跌：市场更关注利润兑现和收⼊质量
财报公布后中国联通股价下跌，并不难理解。
⾸先，收⼊端仅微增，但归⺟净利润和扣⾮归⺟净利润均下滑三成以上，盈利表现弱于收⼊表
现。对运营商这类成熟⾏业公司⽽⾔，市场通常更看重利润稳定性、现⾦分红能⼒和新业务利
润兑现，⽽不仅是收⼊规模。
其次，成本端扩张明显快于收⼊。前三季度营业总成本增加约 119.73 亿元，⽽营业收⼊仅增加
约 16.61 亿元，成本压⼒直接压缩盈利空间。

### Page 4

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。
118 收藏 分享到 :
写评论
图⽚
第三，应收账款较去年末⼤幅增加，也会让市场关注收⼊质量和回款节奏。虽然经营现⾦流仍
保持增⻓，但应收账款、存货上升与合同负债下降同时出现，后续仍需观察。
但也没必要把财报中的正⾯内容全部负⾯化。中国联通的经营现⾦流仍稳健，资本开⽀没有明
显失控，算⼒、国际业务和连接规模仍在增⻓。股价下跌更像是市场在财报窗⼝期对 “ 利润下滑
幅度较⼤、转型业务尚未充分释放利润贡献 ” 的短期定价反应。接下来，投资者⼤概率会继续关
注成本率能否回落、应收账款能否收敛，以及算⼒和 AI 相关业务能否转化为更清晰的利润增⻓。

動態更新  運動 用品  ‧美國

Deckers 
(DECK.O/DECK US)

美國市場疲態顯現 
重要訊息  
Deckers  2Q FY27 (財年止於 3 月；7-9 月) 財報 優於預期 ，但展望 暴露美國
消費前景趨 疲。管理層下調 FY27 營收展望 ，並表示市場庫存調整可能延伸
至 2027 年春夏季。  
評論及分析  
2Q FY27 利潤率為亮點 。2Q FY27 稅後淨利 年增 17%至 3.1 億美元，折合
每股盈餘 2.32 美元，優於市場預期 28%，主因利潤率超前， 且有美國政府
退稅 挹注每股盈餘 0.28 美元。營收年增 4%至 14.8 億美元 ，略低於指引，
係受歐洲第三方物流  (3PL) 轉型導致運輸延誤及部分電商業務疲軟影響。
排除一次性退稅利益，全價銷售、 產品組合優化及 費用控管帶動毛利率及
營利率分別年增、季增至 57.4% 、23.2% ，高於市場預估 。 
美國市場 壓力 漸增 。管理層觀察到美國消費者採購行為轉變，客流量集中
促銷活動期間， 並有延後購買現 象。加上運動鞋領域同業促銷競爭加劇，
公司 堅守價格底線 導致直接面對消費者銷售成長明顯趨緩， 2Q FY27 美國
銷售 再度陷入年減 0.4%。作為應對策略，管理層計畫強化 高端產品在歐美
批發分銷管道布局，預計 2027 年春季至秋季 120 美元以上價格帶產品將
擴大市佔率，但價位低於 120 美元 的產品可能因競爭激烈而表現較疲軟 。 
營收展望 調降。公司下調 FY27 營收展望至年增 4-5% ( 前次為 7-8%)，反
映 Hoka 和 UGG 貢獻分別下修至高、低個位數成長 ，而歐洲 物流延誤需求
將無法完全補上；不過， IEEPA 退稅 仍支撐 FY27 毛利率及營利率財測分別
上調至 58.5% 、23.0% 。短期而言，預計 3Q FY27 營收年增低個位數，遜
於市場預期之高個位數成長 。 未來將 保持庫存緊縮 策略， 並專注全價銷售。 
投資建議  
我們預估 Deckers FY26-28 獲利年複合成長率大幅趨緩至僅 4% (F Y20-26
為 24%) 將壓抑股價動能 ；維持「持有」評等，目標價下調 至 86.6 美元，
基於 FY27-28 平均每股盈餘預估 11 倍，對比歷史本益比區間為 10-35 倍。 
投資風險  
上檔風險為消費較預期強勁及品牌人氣提升。

美國 Deckers

十月 27, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R  2

Deckers  10 月 23 日法說會重點摘要  
 2Q FY27 稅後淨 利年增 17%至 3.1 億 美 元，折合 每股盈餘 2.32 美 元，
優於市場預期 28%， 主因利潤率超前，及美國政府退稅貢獻 每股盈餘
0.28 美元。由於歐洲第三方物流  (3PL) 轉型導致 的運輸延誤 以及部分
電商業務疲軟， 本季 營收低於指引 ，年增 4%、季增 45%至 14.8 億
美元。排除一次性 退稅利 益，毛利率仍年增 1.2、季增 0.9 個百分點
至 57.4% ，主要得益自 全價銷售 及產品組合優化 ；再加上費用控管，
營利率 23.2% 優於市場共識之 20.3%。 
 市場 別營收：美國  (佔總營收 57%) 再度轉弱至年 減 0.4% ，主要受
運動鞋領域促銷活動增加之 影響 ，公司並未參與削價競爭 。國際市場 
(43%) 年增 9%，亞太地區為主要成長來源 ，而歐洲 第三方物流運輸
延誤 造成的負面影響約 2,200 萬美元。  
 品牌別營收： Hoka ( 佔總營收 46%) 年增 7%，批發業務與 DTC 銷售
分別年增 9% (升溫)、 4% (快速 冷卻 ，反映美國市場面臨壓力 )。UGG 
(53%) 年增 4%，批發業務年增 7%，抵銷 DTC 通路 8%衰退。其他
品牌  (1%) 則年減 54%。 
 產品別表現： 核心產品系列銷售 良好，其中以 Bondi 系列最為突出，
Clifton Pro 亦廣受好評。然而，消費者和批發合作夥伴的回饋表明，
今年有部分產品更新效果不如預 期，未來數季將進行一些改善工作。  
 通路別營收：批發業務  (佔總營收 74%) 年增加速至 6%，直接面對
消費者  (DTC ，26%) 則陷入年減 2%。 
 2Q FY27 庫存週轉天數 年增 6%、但季減 2%至 128 天， 仍低於過去
十年同期平均水準之 147 天。公司計畫保持庫存緊縮，並專注於全價
銷售。 
 管理層下調 FY27 營收展望至年增 4-5% (前次為 7-8%)；Hoka 和 UGG
貢獻分別下修至年增 高、低個 位 數。然而，考量 IEEPA 退稅挹注 ，FY27
毛利率及營利率財測則分別上調至 58.5% 、23.0%。加計 FY27 股票
回購 10 億美元，現估 稀釋後每股盈餘落在 7.80-7.95 美元。 
 若不計入 IEEPA 退稅挹注， 公司 預計 FY27 毛利率及營利率 分別約 為
57.0 %、略高於 21.5% ，大致符合市場預期。  
 短期而言，預計 3Q FY27 營收將 年增低個位數， 低於市場預期 ；品牌
別方面， Hoka 、UGG 將分別年增低中、 低個位數。 3Q FY27 稀釋後
每股盈餘預估落在 3.52-3.60 美元 (加計 IEEPA 退稅挹注 0.24 美元) 。 
 Mach 8 已於秋季初上市 ，為迄今為止 Mach 系列中表現最好的一款。
預計 2027 年 1 月推出 Bondi 10 和 Fly Pace，4 月 Mach Pro 接續亮相 。
6 月將 進一步 發售 Bondi Ultra X ，售價 325 美元/雙，其三密度 eTPU
中底提升緩震性能 至全新高度，標誌著 HOKA 創新技術的 重大躍進 。 
 UGG 銷售高峰集中在 11 月底至 12 月初。去年市場環境良好且 天氣
宜人 ，銷售季表現非常強勁，帶動 12 月及 1 月訂單追加； 預計今年
補貨量不會達到去年水準。 
 管理層觀察到消費者行為的 兩大轉變： (1) 消費者越來越追求折扣，

### Page 3

美國 Deckers

十月 27, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R  3

客流量集中在促銷活動期間 ；及 (2) 消費者購買商品更 注重 目的性，
優先考慮 「即買即穿」 和具實用 性的產品 ，甚至等到接近實際需求時
才會購買 (延後購買 )。 
 美國市場感受到較大 壓力。目前消費者會盡可能透過線上批發商購買
產品 ，而這些批發商促銷力道非常 大。公司不願陷入這種過度促銷的
氛圍，因此美國 DTC 銷售成長將低於批發業務。  
 2027 年春季 至秋季，公司將持續 強化美國和歐洲批發分銷管道布局 ，
尤其價位在 120 美元以上的產品可望擴大市佔率， 但價位區間低於
120 美元的產品可能因競爭激烈 而流失市佔率 。 
 截至 2Q FY27 止，公司已回購股票 6.37 億美元，佔已發行 股數 5%，
遠超年初預期；而 剩餘授權額度約 44 億美元。

美國 Deckers

十月 27, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R  4

圖 6: 公司概況  圖 7: UGG 及 Hoka 為主力品牌 
成立於 1973 年， Deckers 為全球生活風格與專業運動鞋類、
服飾及配件領導品牌之一 ， 旗下品牌包括 UGG、Hoka、Teva、 
Koolaburra 和 AHNU 。公司產品銷往 50 多個國家，批發合作
夥伴貢獻 FY2 6 營收 59%，其餘 41%則來自直接面對消費者
銷售  (包括直營店及電商) 。美國為 FY26 營收主要來源地區，
佔比達 58%，但國際市場  (42%) 成長較快。

ESG 圖表定義  
項目 定義 說明 
能源使用 
直接與間接能源消耗總量(十億焦耳) 
- 公司運營範圍內消耗之能源總量 
- 能源使用總量 = 直接能源消耗總量 + 間接能源消耗量 
- 總能源使用量包括購買的能源、生產的能源 
- 就公用事業而言，輸電/電網損耗為其業務活動之一部分，被視為總能源消耗，數據不包括
為滿足能源使用而生產的電力(公用事業為出售而生產) 
- 就公用事業而言，用於能源生產的煤、天然氣或核能等原料不屬於「總能源使用」項下

購買之再生能源 
Primary 再生能源購買總量(十億焦耳) 
- 公司各種來源之能源消耗量與購買的能源中屬於自然界可再生者(太陽能、風能、水能、生
物質能、地熱能)之量 
- 如無證據顯示再生能源由公司生產，則所報告的能源數據視為購買的再生能源

再生能源使用率 再生能源占總能源消耗量之比例  
CO2 約當排放量 
直接 CO2 與 CO2 約當排放量(公噸) 
- 公司擁有或控制的來源的直接排放量(範圍 1 排放量) 
- 相關氣體：二氧化碳(CO2)、甲烷(CH4)、一氧化二氮(N2O)、氫氟碳化物(HFCS)、全氟化合
物(PFCS)、六氟化硫(SF6)、三氟化氮(NF3)

CO2 約當排放量營收比 
直接 CO2 與 CO2 約當排放量(公噸)銷售(百萬元)比 
- 公司擁有或控制的來源的直接排放量(範圍 1 排放量) 
- 相關氣體：二氧化碳(CO2)、甲烷(CH4)、一氧化二氮(N2O)、氫氟碳化物(HFCS)、全氟化合
物(PFCS)、六氟化硫(SF6)、三氟化氮(NF3)

廢棄物總量 
廢棄物總量(公噸) 
- 總廢棄物 = 無害廢棄物 + 有害廢棄物 
- 僅考慮固體廢棄物，但如液體廢棄物以公噸為單位呈報，則會將其加入求得包含液體廢棄
物之總量 
- 對於採礦、石油與天然氣等行業，tailings、廢石、煤、飛灰等廢棄物亦考慮在內

廢棄物回收率 
公司呈報的廢棄物回收率 
- 廢棄物回收率 = 廢棄物回收量/總廢棄物*100 
- 廢棄物轉化為能源或經由廢棄物焚燒產生能源視為廢棄物回收 
- 經由堆肥回收的廢棄物視為回收之廢棄物

總取水量 
總取水量(立方公尺) 
- 由呈報組織直接或經水公司等中介機構從任何水源抽取的總水量 
- 水井、城鎮/公用事業/市政用水、河水、地表水等不同之水源均予以考慮

環保支出 
環保支出總金額 
- 所有用於環境保護，或防止、減少、控制環境因素、影響、危害的投資與支出，亦包括處
置、處理、衛生、清理支出

員工流動率 
員工流動率 
- 包括任何原因(自願或非自願)離開公司的員工，如辭職、退休、自然離職/死亡、醫療失能、
冗員、裁員、重組、解僱、裁減或定期合約到期 
- 員工流動率 = (離開之員工/平均員工人數)*100 
- 平均員工人數 = (本年末員工人數 + 去年末員工人數)/2 
- 本財年末員工數 = 上個財年末員工數 + 新員工數 - 離開之員工數

女性經理 
女性經理百分比 
- 女性經理占公司經理的百分比 
- 如有不同階層之百分比，如最高階、高階、中階、初階，則會考慮中階女性經理之百分比 
- 女性經理百分比 = 女性經理人數/經理總數*100

女性員工 
女性員工百分比 
- 女性員工占公司員工總數的百分比 
- 女性員工百分比 = 女性人數/員工總數*100

教育訓練總時數 
所有員工教育訓練總時數 
- 僅考慮員工教育訓練時數 
- 包括一般員工所有類型的教育訓練(如健康與安全、環境、急難事件救援、技能與職業發展) 
- 如果數據以天為單位，則乘以 8，係假設 1 天 = 8 小時工作

每位員工教育訓練時數 平均每年每位員工總教育訓練時數  
資料來源：Refinitiv，凱基

股價說明  等級  定義  
 增加持股  (OP) 對個股持正面看法，預期個股未來十二個月的表現超越凱基證券集團所追蹤的相關市場的總
報酬。  
 持有  (N) 對個股持中性看法，預期個股未來十二個月的表現符合凱基證券集團所追蹤的相關市場的總
報酬。  
 降低持股  (U) 對個股持負面看法，預期個股未來十二個月的表現低於凱基證券集團所追蹤的相關市場的總
報酬。  
 未評等  (NR)  凱基證券未對該個股加以評等。  
 受法規限制  
未評等  (R) 
受凱基證券集團內部政策和 /或相關法令限制使凱基證券集團無法進行某些形式的資訊交
流，其中包括提供評等給投資人參考。  
  *總報酬 = (十二個月目標價 -現價)/現價

Source PDF: all report pdf/出生世代別にみる家計金融資産の増加 ～インフレ転換で変わる日本の家計②～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-10-28
Pages: 5

### Page 1

Economic Trends

2026 年 10 月 28 日

1/5 
出生世代別にみる家計金融資産の増加 
～インフレ転換で変わる日本の家計②～ 
経済調査部 主席エコノミスト 星野 卓也 
（℡：050-5474-7497）

（要旨）

5 年で変わった家計の投資と金融資産 
前回執筆した「 節約志向はどこに顕れているのか？～インフレ転換で変わる日本の家計 ①～」
（2026 年 10 月 5 日）」に続き、今回も総務省の「全国家計構造調査」を中心にインフレ下での日
本の家計行動の変化を分析していく。今回は家計の金融資産に着目したい。2019 年から 2024 年に
かけての家計環境の変化として、 インフレとともに生じた投資ブームが挙げられる。 インフレによ
って預貯金の実質目減りが強く意識されたことに加え、 円安進行は投資信託などを通じて海外資産
への投資を家計に促した。2024 年からスタートした新NISA は税制優遇を通じてこうした動きを後
押ししている。

各年代で加速する金融資産の蓄積：ただし50 代は例外 
 2019 年から 2024 年にかけて金融資産の平均残高は幅広い世代で増えた。図表１では全国家計構
造調査とその前身である全国消費実態調査のデータを用いて疑似コーホート分析を行い、各世代の
○ 2019 年から 2024 年にかけて、インフレ・株高・新NISA などを背景に、ほぼすべての出生世代で
平均金融資産が従来の蓄積パスを上回って増加した。 疑似コーホートでみると、 各世代の軌跡がそ
ろって上方にシフトしている。 
○ もっとも、 この加速は世代一律ではない。 所得環境が厳しい就職氷河期世代 （2024 年時点で50 代
前半） は、 賃上げによる所得増が物価高による消費増に相殺され、 黒字の積み増しが鈍く、 資産蓄
積の加速に出遅れる形に。 
○ 金融資産の 「中身」 も変わった。 有価証券比率は全世代で上昇したが、 とりわけ若年層の上昇が急
ピッチで、1980～84 年生まれは 5 年間で約 9％から約 25％へ跳ね上がった。預貯金中心から証券
保有へと資産形成の形が変わりつつある。 
○ ただし、平均の増加は分布の上側に偏在している。多くの年代で上位10％の境界額（第 9 十分位
数） が中央値を大きく上回って伸びており、 平均値の上昇を世帯全般の豊かさと読むことはできな
い。資産格差は緩やかに広がっている。

### Page 2

2/5 
平均金融資産の動向をみたものである。色で出生年代（世代）を分け、各出生年代の各年齢時点の
平均金融資産を表している。各プロットをつないでいくと現役時に金融資産を積み上げ、 老齢期に
金融資産残高が減少する（非常に緩やかではあるが）形となっており、教科書的なライフサイクル
仮説に概ね沿った姿となる。 
 グラフ上の各世代（各色）の推移が概ね重なっていることは、各世代が同じようなペースで金融
資産の蓄積を進めてきたことを示す。しかし、最新調査の 2024 年時点では多くの世代で従来の金
融資産蓄積パスからの明確な上振れがみられた。 特に40 代前半と60 代前半に至る世代の直近の増
加が目立つ。2024 年の平均金融資産残高は、40～44 歳で約 1,091 万円、60～64 歳で約 2,379 万円
となり、 それぞれ10 年前の先輩が同じ年齢だった時点を約292 万円、 約441 万円上回った。また、
現在 70～74 歳の世代の金融資産残高は5 年前から増加した。5 年先輩世代が、同時期に平均金融資
産残高を減少させていたことと対照的である。80～84 歳の平均金融資産残高も5 年前調査（75～79
歳）の値を上回った。総じて、この5 年間で多くの世代が先輩世代よりも早いペースで金融資産の
蓄積を進めたことを示している。賃金上昇、老後不安の高まり、それに根差した投資ブームによる
リスク性資産へのシフトと株高などが各世帯の金融資産残高の増加要因となっている。

図表１．出生世代別・平均金融資産の変化（5 年毎調査を用いた疑似コーホート）

（注）金額は 1 世帯当たり名目残高。各調査時点における年齢別平均金融資産残高を用いて疑似コーホート
分析。各点は別標本の 5 歳階級平均で同一世帯の追跡ではない点に留意。2004～2014 年は 2019 年方式によ
る公式遡及集計。白抜きは直近 2024 年調査。 
（出所）総務省「全国消費実態調査」「全国家計構造調査」より第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所が作成。

### Page 3

3/5 
一方、特徴的なのは50～54 歳である。2024 年時点の平均金融資産残高は約 1,396 万円で、10 年
前の先輩の約1,384 万円とほぼ重なった。 この世代自身の金融資産も直近5 年間で約373 万円増え
ているが、先輩世代の蓄積パスを大きく上回るほどの増加にはなっていない。 
こうした 50 代の出遅れの背景は、家計収支の面からもうかがえる。黒字（可処分所得－消費支
出） を計算できる勤労者世帯について、45～49 歳から50～54 歳への5 年間を、10 年前に同じ年齢
を通った先輩世代と比べてみる。賃上げの進んだ今回世代は可処分所得の増加額こそ 10 年先輩世
代を上回ったものの、物価上昇によって消費の増加額が先輩以上に膨らんだため、 月額の黒字の増
え方は先輩世代：約 1.3 万円に対して約0.5 万円にとどまった。 所得が増えても黒字の積み増しに
結び付く程度は弱まっており、所得環境が相対的に厳しかったとされる「就職氷河期世代」が、金
融資産の拡大トレンドに出遅れている様子が見て取れる。

若い世代の有価証券比率が急ピッチで上昇 
金融資産の増加を読むうえでは、 残高だけでなくその中身も重要である。 そこで有価証券比率に
ついて同様の疑似コーホート分析を行ったものが図表２である。図表 2 は図表1 と同じ出生世代・
年齢軸で、有価証券が金融資産に占める割合を示した。

図表２．出生世代別・有価証券比率の変化（5 年毎調査を用いた疑似コーホート）

（注） 有価証券比率＝有価証券残高÷金融資産残高×100。 各点は別標本の5 歳階級平均で同一世帯の追跡ではない。2004
～2014 年は2019 年方式による公式遡及集計。 
（出所）総務省「全国消費実態調査」「全国家計構造調査」より第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所が作成。

### Page 4

4/5 
近年では、 図に示したすべての世代で有価証券比率が上昇している。 なかでも上昇が急なのが若
い世代だ。1980～84 年生まれは、35～39 歳時点（2019 年）の約 9％から 40～44 歳時点（2024 年）
の約 25％へと、わずか5 年で比率をほぼ3 倍に高めた。50 代前半に至る1970～74 年生まれも、45
～49 歳時点の約14％から約 22％へ上昇しており、金融資産残高の蓄積では出遅れたこの世代も、
資産構成の面では着実にリスク性資産を厚くしている。 
上昇は若年層に限らない。1950 年生まれ（2024 年に 70～74 歳）は約 19％から約 25％へ、1940
年生まれ（同80～84 歳）も約17％から約22％へと高まった。有価証券比率の上昇には、新規の買
い付けだけでなく保有資産の値上がり（評価益）も含まれる点には留意が必要であるが、家計のポ
ートフォリオがインフレ下での預貯金の実質目減りや株高、新 NISA を通じて大きく変化したこと
が読み取れる。

金融資産の蓄積は上位層に偏重している 
ただし、この平均金融資産の増加は、必ずしも世代内で均一に生じているわけではない。図表３
は、 同じ年齢層について2019 年と2024 年の金融資産分布を箱ひげ図で比較したものである。 箱は
第1～第3 四分位（p25～p75）、箱中の線は中央値、ひげの両端は第1 十分位（p10）と第9 十分位
（p90） を表す。 各年代とも、 箱やひげが上方向に伸びる一方、 下端 （p10・p25） はほとんど動いて
おらず、分布が上側へ広がったことが読み取れる。

図表３．箱ひげ図による2019 年・2024 年の各年代別金融資産分布の比較

（注） 箱＝第1～第 3 四分位 （p25～p75） 、 中央線＝中央値、 ひげ＝第1 十分位 （p10） と第9 十分位 （p90）、◇＝平均。
分位数で作図しており実データの最小・最大ではない。同じ年齢層の比較であり、同一出生世代の追跡ではない。 
（出所）総務省「全国家計構造調査」より第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所が作成。

### Page 5

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
5/5 
例えば 55～64 歳では、平均が約 369 万円増えた。一方、中央値の増加は約 44 万円にとどまり、
上位 10％の境界額（p90）は約880 万円増加した。35～44 歳では平均は約305 万円増だが、中央値
は約 133 万円増にとどまる。p90 は約 865 万円増と突出している。全世帯でみても、中央値の増加
が約 78 万円に対しp90 は約 520 万円増と、分布の上側ほど大きく伸びた。 
一方で、 上側がどの年代でも大きく動いたわけではない。45～54 歳 （就職氷河期世代を含む） は、
平均（約143 万円増） も上位10％境界額 （約248 万円増） も伸びが相対的に小さく、図表１でみた
蓄積の出遅れと整合的である。 
以上ではインフレ転換後の家計金融資産の動向について世代・年齢別に分析を行った。第一に、
2019 年から 2024 年にかけて、多くの世代が過去の蓄積パスを上回るペースで金融資産を積み増し
た。株高も相まって、 有価証券の比率が高まっており、若年層の上昇は急速である。 賃上げ・ 株高 ・
新NISA という追い風が、世代を超えて家計のバランスシートを膨らませている。 
もっとも、 その恩恵は一様ではない。 世代間でみると、所得環境の厳しかった就職氷河期世代 （50
代前半）については、賃上げが物価高と消費増に吸収されており、他世代で見られた蓄積ペース加
速に出遅れる形となった。また、世代内でみるといずれの世代でも残高の増加は中央値よりも分布
の上側でより大きくなっており、 相対的に資産を多く持つ層に偏っている。 インフレ転換と経済環
境の変化は家計金融資産の増加を促す一方で、世代間・世代内でばらつき、資産格差を静かに広げ
ている。

以上

[Table_Summary] 核心观点： 
1) 权益：上周市场缩量下跌。①市场方面， 上周海外市场大幅波
动，AI 融资担忧持续发酵避险情绪升温，中东地缘扰动推升
滞胀担忧， 权益市场受海外风偏回落传导整体下行， 周五主力
资金维稳止跌。②基本面方面，三季度 GDP 增长 4.5%超市场
预期， 全年完成目标压力较小， 基本面预期上修， 顺周期逻辑
强化， 政策预期下修， 但核心增量政策9 月末已出台， 整体影
响有限。9 月经济数据整体向好， 出口高增与投资修复是核心
驱动。 固定投资环比为连续3 月首次转正， 主要是基建与制造
业投资带动， 地产投资与销售增速均降幅扩大， 后续购房贴息
政策的出台短期对后续地产数据预计边际有所改善，10 月地
方债务限额启动下达，PSL 降息扩围， 投资修复线索四季度具
备延续性。 消费受中秋国庆假期提前带动， 剔除汽车社零同比
回升较为显著。 
2) 固收：上周债市收益率短端下行长端调整。①当周催化方面，
资金维持宽松态势，虽然 MLF 缩量续作但 10 月份“买断式回
购+MLF”整体超额续作 2000 亿元，短端利率下行，但上周大
类资本市场波动剧烈，权益市场下行，固收+赎回压力下，30
年期流动性补充叠加止盈压力下利率有所上行。 ②后期来看，
随着政府债发行高峰逐步过去， 央行对资金面呵护程度可能边
际改变， 且经济基本面边际回升， 存量政策落地逐步发挥作用，
叠加债券年内收益较为丰厚， 利率处在低位， 兑现收益止盈需
求较强， 债市风险有所抬升。 短期或仍在利多通道， 可随利率
下行缩短久期。 
3) 海外：上周美股下行、 美债利率下行、 美元指数下行、 人民币
升值、黄金下跌、油价上行。① AI 融资担忧持续发酵，Meta
支出承诺升至 8250 亿美元， 但自由现金流转负加大融资担忧，
英伟达指出营运资金不足，此前引发长期美债利率上行的 AI
融资竞争压力弱化， 且增长担忧下， 美联储加息预期回落， 叠
加科技股下跌，市场波动放大避险需求，美债利率明显下行，
尽管中东地缘扰动推升油价， 但美债开始定价滞胀担忧。 黄金
因油价滞胀压力、 美债信用压力缓释、 流动性需求而下行， 但
目前加息预期回落下，性价比相对凸显。 
4) 风险提示：海外地缘政治风险超预期、 美联储加息路径超预期。

三、风险提示 
①海外地缘政治风险超预期。若美伊局势持续升级导致油价持续高企，通胀
预期将导致全球长债利率持续高位，压制市场风偏。

②美联储加息路径超预期。当前点阵图预期年内 2 次加息，若油价持续攀升，
市场对加息预期走高推动美联储加息路径超预期，科技资本融资将受到更加
明显压制，市场中期风偏将受到更为明显的压制。

分析师声明 
本报告署名分析师郑重声明：本人以勤勉的职业态度，独立、客观地出具本报告，保证报告所采用的数据和信息均来
自公开合规渠道，报告的分析逻辑基于本人职业理解，报告清晰准确地反映了本人的研究观点，结论不受任何第三方
的授意或影响。本人薪酬的任何部分不曾有，不与，也将不会与本报告中的具体推荐意见或观点直接或间接相关。

证券研究报告对研究对象的评价是本人通过财务分析预测、数量化方法、行业比较分析、估值分析等方式所得出的结
论，但使用以上信息和分析方法存在局限性。特此声明。

投资评级标准

1 
PTFE 材料影響性評析

報告日期： 2026/10/19 研 究 員：方姿婷  (Lesley Fang)  
新 聞 評 析 
2026.10.15(四)新聞重點摘要： 
NVIDIA 於 2026 年 10 月中旬 OCP 大會上，展示 MGX 伺服器平
台生態系最新進展，更首度公開導入 PTFE( 聚四氟乙烯 )材料的交
換器板 (Switch board) 與相關台廠供應鏈名單。

一. 輝達 OCP 展示 MGX 平台導入 PTFE( 聚四氟乙烯 ) 
NVIDIA 於 OCP Global Summit 2026 上，展示 MGX 伺服器平台
生態系最新進展，更首度公開導入 PTFE( 聚四氟乙烯 )材料的交換
器板 (switch board) 樣品，並欽點 7 家供應鏈合作夥伴，台廠有台
郡(6269) 、台虹 (8039)2 家業者入列，其餘為，中系廠商勝宏、深
南電路、景旺、生益，以及來自美國的迅達 (TTM) 。

二. PTFE 材料特性與 CCL 材料比較，後段加工為關鍵  
宏遠投顧觀察 PTFE(Polytetrafluoroethylene ， 聚四氟乙烯)擁有極
低介電常數、以及極低介電損耗 (在 10 GHz 頻率之下， PTFE Dk
值= 2.1 、Df 值=0.0003) 。此強調極低損耗的聚合物材料，使得
PTFE 性能優於 PPO/PPE 與標準碳氫樹脂 。PTFE 材料擁有出色
的化學穩定性與耐腐蝕性，氟原子均勻分佈於碳主鏈周圍，鍵結
強度是 PTFE 其「穩定性」來源， PTFE 特性抵抗熱劣化、氧化與
化學侵蝕的能力，遠優於碳氫或 PPO 分子，因此，在 AI 伺服器
平台對於高速、高頻傳輸要求之下， PTFE 介電性能具有優勢。然
而， PCB 後段加工會經歷多次壓合的製程， PTFE 材料會面臨諸
多挑戰，由於 PTFE 屬於 Thermoplastic( 熱塑性材料 )，PTFE 材
料較難承受多次層壓 /壓合與 PCBA 製程循環。相對於 PTFE ，銅
箔基板當中常見之， Epoxy( 環氧樹脂，具有高強度黏著、防水防
潮、耐化學腐蝕、優異之電氣特性與絕緣效果 )、PPO/PPE 與碳
氫樹脂屬於 Thermosetting resin( 熱固性樹脂 )， 熱固性樹脂類型產
品，加熱到一定溫度後，皆具備強度高、耐酸鹼、抗腐蝕等優勢，
可於短時間內完成快速且緻密的固化反應，相對適合量產型產線
與加工。

三. 維持台光電 /台燿等高階銅箔基板廠商買進看法  
綜合上述， PTFE 與傳統 CCL 材料特性之比較。即使未來 NVIDIA 
VR(Rubin Ultra) 系列規格設計上，在交換器 CCL 材料供應，可能
會採用 M8 或 M9 與 PTFE 混搭設計，市場盛傳台光電 (2383) 與
相關個股：台光電 
投資評等：買進

     32 X 2027 EPS

     25.2 X 2027EPS

     31 X 2027 EPS

     24.4 X 2027EPS

Document 651: 对冲基金这个动作，正在放大科技股风险！

Source PDF: all report pdf/对冲基金这个动作，正在放大科技股风险！.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 3

### Page 1

对冲基⾦正悄然完成⼀场危险的策略切换 —— 从做空个股转向做空指数。这⼀转变正推动
股票相关性加速攀升，昔⽇压低波动率的 " 反杠杆 " 缓冲正在消失。⾼盛数据显⽰，基⾦对指
数及 ETF 的空头敞⼝已达 10 年⾼位。 VIX ⾯临⽆序跳升⻛险，股价深度下跌的脆弱性正悄然
积聚。
摘要：
对冲基⾦正悄然改变其对冲策略，这⼀转变正在为科技股市场埋下更⼤的不稳定隐患。
周五，据彭博宏观策略师 Simon White 分析，对冲基⾦已从主要通过做空个股来对冲⼈⼯智能多
头敞⼝，转向越来越多地做空指数。这⼀策略切换正在推⾼股票相关性，令 VIX 出现⽆序跳升的
⻛险上升，市场⾯临价格急剧下跌的潜在威胁。
10 ⽉份，科技板块遭遇约 8% 的回撤， AI 相关仓位被⼤规模平仓，众多基⾦损失惨重。尽管如
此，在资本开⽀热潮持续、盈利预期依然⾼企的背景下，基⾦经理们普遍不愿过早退出 AI 交易。
为在保留多头仓位的同时满⾜主经纪商的降杠杆要求，基⾦选择增加做空来压低净敞⼝ —— ⽽
做空的标的，正越来越多地指向科技股指数本⾝。

从做空个股到做空指数，策略悄然切换
来⾃⾼盛的数据显⽰，近期对冲基⾦总仓位（ gross position ）急剧攀升，⽽净仓位（ net
position ）则⼤幅下滑，这⼀背离清晰勾勒出基⾦在保留多头的同时⼤举增加空头的操作路径。
据彭博报道，利⽤美国证券交易委员会（ SEC ） Form PF 披露的对冲基⾦资产负债表数据（最
新数据截⾄今年第⼆季度），可以印证上述趋势：⾃ 2024 年以来，多空股票基⾦空头账簿的增速
已明显超过多头账簿。股票多空基⾦的总敞⼝⾃ 2024 年以来增加逾 5 万亿美元，升⾄逾 8 万亿美
元，⽽净敞⼝仅增加约 1 万亿美元⾄ 2 万亿美元。
在具体⼯具选择上，对冲基⾦倾向于使⽤⾼流动性股票 ETF 进⾏指数层⾯的对冲。追踪纳斯达克
100 指数的 QQQ ETF 空头兴趣今年以来显著攀升，尤其在过去两周出现额外跳升；⽽追踪标普
500 指数的 SPY ETF 空头兴趣则相对平稳。

### Page 2

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂

写评论
这⼀分化表明，基⾦针对的正是科技与 AI 仓位 ——SPY 对这类敞⼝的代理效果不够精准， QQQ
才是更贴近的对冲⼯具。⾼盛数据亦显⽰，截⾄今年 5 ⽉，对冲基⾦对美国股票宏观产品（包括
指数和 ETF ）的空头敞⼝已达 10 年⾼位。
相关性上升， " 反杠杆 " 效应正在消退
这⼀策略转变的深层影响，在于其对股票相关性的冲击。历史数据显⽰，空头兴趣与股票相关
性之间存在稳定的正向关系：当⼤规模做空覆盖全市场个股时，股票倾向于同向运动，相关性
随之上升。
此前，对冲基⾦主要通过做空个股进⾏对冲，这⼀⾏为客观上压低了指数层⾯的相关性，使得
即便个股⻛险上升，指数波动率依然保持低位 —— 这相当于为科技公司因 AI 资本开⽀⼤举举债所
积累的资产负债表⻛险，提供了⼀层 " 反杠杆 " 缓冲， VIX 也因此维持在相对温和的⽔平。

然⽽，今年以来， AI 个股期权的投机性买盘逐渐降温，半导体与软件股之间的相对价值交易等策
略相继爆仓并被平仓，单股波动率的优势不再，指数层⾯的对冲操作 —— 通过 ETF 空头、指数期
货及看跌期权 —— 开始主导市场，推动股票相关性重新⾛⾼。科技股相关性与 QQQ 空头兴趣的
背离已然修复，⼆者再度同步上⾏。
VIX 偏向上⾏，价格下⾏⻛险加剧
上述机制的叠加，意味着市场结构正在发⽣对投资者不利的转变。
当股票相关性处于低位时，指数层⾯的⻛险可以被个股之间的分散效应所抵消， VIX 得以保持低
迷。但随着相关性回升⾄更正常的⽔平，科技公司资产负债表上因 AI 资本开⽀⽽积累的债务⻛险
将更充分地暴露在 VIX 之中，令后者⾯临向上冲击的压⼒。
对于股票选股者⽽⾔，相关性上升意味着个股分散化的价值下降，市场出现系统性波动时，损
失将更为普遍。对于整体市场⽽⾔，股价对于深度下跌的脆弱性正在上升。
Simon White 总结指出，对冲基⾦正在坚守其核⼼ AI 多头仓位，但压缩净敞⼝的⽅式已从做空个

股转向做空指数。曾经压低相关性的顺⻛，如今正在转变为推⾼相关性的顺⻛。 VIX 存在上⾏偏
向，股价则更易遭受更⼤幅度的下跌。

Document 673: 指引怎樣爆雷了？CPU不是很好賣嗎？ 財報解剖_ Intel 英特爾_ 26年10月

Source PDF: all report pdf/指引怎樣爆雷了？CPU不是很好賣嗎？ 財報解剖_ Intel 英特爾_ 26年10月.pdf

### Page 1

From:    KP - FOMO 研究院  <fomosoc@substack.com>
Subject: 指引怎樣爆雷了？ CPU 不是很好賣嗎？  財報解剖 : Intel 英特爾 | 26 年 10 ⽉

指引怎樣爆雷了？ CPU 不是很好賣嗎？  財報解剖 : Intel 英特爾 | 26 年 10 ⽉
